# Godot WRY

[WRY](https://github.com/tauri-apps/wry) is a cross-platform webview rendering library. This extension allows you to use the native webview in Godot to build browsers and GUIs with HTML, CSS and JavaScript.

## ✨ Features

- 🍃 Use the native system native webview (no extra dependencies!)
- 🌎 Load website URLs and local `res://` files
- 🧩 JavaScript ⇔ GDScript code integration
- 🚥 Mouse/keyboard input events forwarding

## ⛹️ Demo

<p align="center">
  <img src="assets/demo-cas.gif">
  Demo game UI available at "<a href="godot/addons/godot_wry/examples/character_creator_ui_demo">examples/character_creator_ui_demo</a>".
</p>

<details>
  <summary>📸 Other screenshots</summary>
  
  ![](assets/screenshot-7.png)
  ![](assets/screenshot-6.png)
  ![](assets/screenshot-4.png)
  ![](assets/screenshot-5.png)
  
</details>

## 💾 Installing

### Asset Library

The easiest way to install Godot WRY is through Godot's [Asset Library](https://godotengine.org/asset-library/asset/3426). You can install it via the editor by following these instructions:

1. Open your project in Godot 4.1 or later.
2. Go to the "📥 AssetLib" tab at the top of the editor.
3. Search for "Godot WRY".
4. Click on the Godot WRY extension and click **Download**.
5. In the configuration dialog, click **Install**.

### GitHub releases

1. Go to the [Releases](https://github.com/doceazedo/godot_wry/releases) page.
2. Download the latest release ZIP file (_not_ the source code).
3. Extract the contents into your project's "addons" folder (create one if it doesn't exist yet).

### Build from source

Use [just](https://github.com/casey/just) to build the extension and move the binaries to the Godot project folder:

```sh
$ just build
```

If you need a more in-depth guide on how to compile the project, check the [Building from source](https://godot-wry.doce.sh/contributing/compiling.html) documentation page.

## 📚 Documentation

Please refer to the [Docs](https://godot-wry.doce.sh) for API reference and in-depth guides on how to use Godot WRY.

## 🎯 Supported platforms

| Platform                        | Support        | Web engine                 |
| ------------------------------- | -------------- | -------------------------- |
| **Windows (10, 11)**            | ✅ Supported   | WebView2 (Chromium)        |
| **Mac (Intel, Apple Sillicon)** | ✅ Supported   | WebKit                     |
| **Linux (X11)**                 | 🚧 Supported\* | WebKitGTK                  |
| **Android**                     | ⏳ Planned     | Android WebView (Chromium) |
| **iOS**                         | ⏳ Planned     | WebKit                     |
| **Browser/HTML5**               | ⏳ Planned     | —                          |

### Linux

[WebKitGTK](https://webkitgtk.org) is required for WRY to function on Linux. The package name may differ based on the operating system and Linux distribution.

\* X11 support only. Transparency is currently not supported. See [#17](https://github.com/doceazedo/godot_wry/issues/17).

### Android/iOS

WRY itself already has [mobile support](https://github.com/tauri-apps/wry/blob/dev/MOBILE.md). Contributions to add Android/iOS support in this extension are welcome!

## ❌ Caveats

- Webview always renders on top
- Different browser engines across platforms
- No automatic dependency checks

You can learn more about these caveats on the [Caveats](https://godot-wry.doce.sh/about/caveats.html) documentation page.

## 🤝 Contribute

Your help is most welcome regardless of form! Check out the [How to contribute](https://godot-wry.doce.sh/contributing/how-to-contribute.html) page for all ways you can contribute to the project. For example, [suggest a new feature](https://github.com/doceazedo/godot_wry/issues/new?template=feature_request.md), [report a problem/bug](https://github.com/doceazedo/godot_wry/issues/new?template=bug_report.md), [submit a pull request](https://help.github.com/en/github/collaborating-with-issues-and-pull-requests/about-pull-requests), or simply use the project and comment your experience.

See the [Roadmap](https://godot-wry.doce.sh/about/roadmap.html) documentation page for an idea of how the project should evolve.

## 🎫 License

The Godot WRY extension is licensed under [MIT](/LICENSE). WRY is licensed under [Apache-2.0/MIT](https://github.com/tauri-apps/wry/blob/dev/LICENSE.spdx).

## 🧪 Similar projects

Below is a list of interesting similar projects:

- [gdcef](https://github.com/Lecrapouille/gdcef/tree/godot-4.x) — Open-source, powered by Chromium (CEF)
- [Godot-HTML](https://github.com/Decapitated/Godot-HTML) — Open-source, powered by Ultralight (WebKit)
- [godot-webview](https://godotwebview.com/) — Commercial, powered by Qt6 (Chromium)

543:         self.base().clone().connect("resized", &Callable::from_object_method(&*self.base(), "resize"));
544:         self.base().clone().connect("visibility_changed", &Callable::from_object_method(&*self.base(), "update_visibility"));
545:     }
546: 
547:     fn reparent_webview(&mut self, new_window_id: i32) {
548:         if self.webview.is_none() { return; }
549: 
550:         #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
551:         {
552:             let window = GodotWindow::new(new_window_id);
553:             if let Ok(wh) = window.window_handle() {
554:                 if let RawWindowHandle::Win32(win32) = wh.as_raw() {
555:                     let hwnd = win32.hwnd.get() as isize;
556: 
557:                     unsafe {
558:                         let raw_hwnd = HWND(hwnd as _);
559:                         let current_style = GetWindowLongPtrA(raw_hwnd, GWL_STYLE);
560:                         SetWindowLongPtrA(raw_hwnd, GWL_STYLE, current_style & !0x02000000);
561:                     };
562: 
563:                     if self.webview.as_ref().unwrap().reparent(hwnd).is_ok() {
564:                         self.window_id = new_window_id;
565:                         self.resize();
566:                         return;
567:                     }
568:                 }
569:             }
570:             godot_warn!("[Godot WRY] Native reparent failed, falling back to rebuild");
571:         }
572: 
573:         self.webview.take();
574:         self.build_webview();
575:     }

579:         if let Some(webview) = &self.webview {
580:             let data = serde_json::json!({ "detail": String::from(message) });
581:             let script = format!("document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('message', {}))", data);
582:             let _ = webview.evaluate_script(&script);
583:         }
584:     }

586:     #[func]
587:     fn resize(&self) {
588:         if let Some(webview) = &self.webview {
589:             let rect = if self.full_window_size {
590:                 let window_size = self.base().get_window()
591:                     .map(|w| w.get_size())
592:                     .unwrap_or_else(|| {
593:                         self.base().get_tree().expect("Could not get tree")
594:                             .get_root().expect("Could not get viewport").get_size()
595:                     });
596:                 Rect {
597:                     position: PhysicalPosition::new(0, 0).into(),
598:                     size: PhysicalSize::new(window_size.x, window_size.y).into(),
599:                 }
600:             } else {
601:                 let pos = self.base().get_global_position();
602:                 let size = self.base().get_size();
603:                 let (scale_x, scale_y) = self.get_content_scale();
604:                 let phys_x = (pos.x * scale_x).round();
605:                 let phys_y = (pos.y * scale_y).round();
606:                 Rect {
607:                     position: PhysicalPosition::new(phys_x, phys_y).into(),
608:                     size: PhysicalSize::new(size.x * scale_x, size.y * scale_y).into(),
609:                 }
610:             };
611:             let _ = webview.set_bounds(rect);
612:         }
613:     }

615:     fn get_content_scale(&self) -> (f32, f32) {
616:         if let Some(window) = self.base().get_window() {
617:             let window_size = window.get_size();
618:             if let Some(viewport) = self.base().get_viewport() {
619:                 let vp_size = viewport.get_visible_rect().size;
620:                 if vp_size.x > 0.0 && vp_size.y > 0.0 {
621:                     return (
622:                         window_size.x as f32 / vp_size.x,
623:                         window_size.y as f32 / vp_size.y,
624:                     );
625:                 }
626:             }
627:         }
628:         (1.0, 1.0)
629:     }

756: fn send_wheel_event(
757:     button: MouseButton,
758:     position: Vector2,
759:     factor: f32,
760:     button_mask: MouseButtonMask,
761:     modifiers: (bool, bool, bool, bool),
762:     viewport: &Option<Gd<Viewport>>,

772:         event.set_button_mask(button_mask);
773:         event.set_shift_pressed(shift);
774:         event.set_ctrl_pressed(ctrl);
775:         event.set_alt_pressed(alt);
776:         event.set_meta_pressed(meta);
777:         if let Some(vp) = viewport {
778:             vp.clone().push_input(&event);
779:         }
780:     }
781: }

11: pub fn get_res_response(request: Request<Vec<u8>>) -> Response<Cow<'static, [u8]>> {
12:     let root = PathBuf::from("res://");
13:     let uri = request.uri().clone();
14:     let path = format!(
15:         "{}{}",
16:         uri.host().unwrap_or_default(),
17:         uri.path()
18:     );
19:     let mut full_path = root.join(&path);
20: 
21:     debug_print!("[WRY Protocol] Request: {} | scheme={} host={} path={}", uri, uri.scheme_str().unwrap_or("?"), uri.host().unwrap_or("?"), uri.path());
22:     debug_print!("[WRY Protocol] Resolved full_path: {:?}", full_path);
23: 
24:     let mut full_path_str = GString::from(full_path.to_str().unwrap_or_default());
25:     if !FileAccess::file_exists(&full_path_str) {
26:         debug_print!("[WRY Protocol] File not found: {:?}, trying index.html fallback", full_path);
27:         let index_path = full_path.join("index.html");
28:         let index_path_str = GString::from(index_path.to_str().unwrap_or_default());
29:         if FileAccess::file_exists(&index_path_str) {
30:             if !uri.path().ends_with('/') {
31:                 let redirect_url = format!(
32:                     "http://res.{}{}/",
33:                     uri.host().unwrap_or_default(),
34:                     uri.path()
35:                 );
36:                 debug_print!("[WRY Protocol] No trailing slash, JS redirect -> {}", redirect_url);
37:                 let redirect_html = format!(
38:                     "<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><script>location.replace(\"{}\")</script></head></html>",
39:                     redirect_url
40:                 );
41:                 return http::Response::builder()
42:                     .header(CONTENT_TYPE, "text/html")
43:                     .status(200)
44:                     .body(Cow::from(redirect_html.into_bytes()))
45:                     .expect("Failed to build redirect response");
46:             } else {
47:                 debug_print!("[WRY Protocol] Trailing slash present, serving index.html directly: {:?}", index_path);
48:             }
49:             full_path = index_path;
50:             full_path_str = index_path_str;
51:         }
52:     }
53: 
54:     if !FileAccess::file_exists(&full_path_str) {
55:         debug_print!("[WRY Protocol] 404 Not Found: {:?}", full_path);
56:         return http::Response::builder()
57:             .header(CONTENT_TYPE, "text/plain")
58:             .status(404)
59:             .body(Cow::from(
60:                 format!("Could not find file at {:?}", full_path)
61:                     .as_bytes()
62:                     .to_vec(),
63:             ))
64:             .expect("Failed to build 404 response");
65:     }
66: 
67:     let extension = full_path
68:             .extension()
69:             .unwrap_or_default()
70:             .to_str()
71:             .unwrap_or_default();
72: 
73:     let content_type = MIME_TYPES
74:             .get(extension)
75:             .unwrap_or(&"application/octet-stream");
76: 
77:     return FileAccess::open(&full_path_str, ModeFlags::READ)
78:         .map(|mut file| {
79:             let file_size: u64 = file.get_length().try_into().expect("failed to get file size");
80: 
81:             // The client might request a file with Range,
82:             // even if we set Accept-Ranges to none, Safari does this while loading media types.
83:             // So, we MUST implement the Content-Range logic to serve the file correctly.
84:             let mut content_range: Option<(u64, u64)> = None;
85:             if let Some(range) = request.headers().get(RANGE) {
86:                 let range_str = range.to_str().expect("failed to parse Range header");
87: 
88:                 // the range header might be in the format "bytes=start-end", "bytes=start-", or "bytes=-end"
89:                 let parts: Vec<&str> = range_str[6..].split('-').collect();
90:                 let start = parts[0].parse::<u64>().unwrap_or(0);
91:                 let end = parts[1].parse::<u64>().unwrap_or(file_size - 1);
92:                 content_range = Some((start, end));
93:             }
94: 
95:             return if let Some((start, end)) = content_range {
96:                 if start >= file_size {
97:                     return http::Response::builder()
98:                         .header(CONTENT_TYPE, *content_type)
99:                         .header(ACCEPT_RANGES, "bytes")
100:                         .header(CONTENT_RANGE, format!("bytes */{}", file_size))
101:                         .status(416) // Range Not Satisfiable
102:                         .body(Cow::from(Vec::new()))
103:                         .expect("Failed to build 416 response");
104:                 }
105: 
106:                 let end = if end == 0 || end >= file_size {
107:                     file_size - 1
108:                 } else {
109:                     end
110:                 };
111: 
112:                 let content_size = (end - start + 1) as i64;
113:                 file.seek(start);
114:                 let content = file.get_buffer(content_size).as_slice().to_vec();
115: 
116:                 http::Response::builder()
117:                     .header(CONTENT_TYPE, *content_type)
118:                     .header(ACCEPT_RANGES, "bytes")
119:                     .header(CONTENT_RANGE, format!("bytes {}-{}/{}", start, end, file_size))
120:                     .status(206)
121:                     .body(Cow::from(content))
122:                     .expect("Failed to build 206 response")
123:             } else {
124:                 let content_size = file_size as i64;
125:                 let content = file.get_buffer(content_size).as_slice().to_vec();
126:                 http::Response::builder()
127:                     .header(CONTENT_TYPE, *content_type)
128:                     .status(200)
129:                     .body(Cow::from(content))
130:                     .expect("Failed to build 200 response")
131:             }
132:         }).unwrap_or_else(|| {
133:             http::Response::builder()
134:                 .header(CONTENT_TYPE, "text/plain")
135:                 .status(500)
136:                 .body(Cow::from(
137:                     format!("Failed to open at {:?}", full_path)
138:                         .as_bytes()
139:                         .to_vec(),
140:                 ))
141:                 .expect("Failed to build 404 response")
142:         });
143: }
144: 
145: lazy_static! {
146:     static ref MIME_TYPES: HashMap<&'static str, &'static str> = HashMap::from([
147:         // https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Guides/MIME_types/Common_types
148:         ("aac", "audio/aac"),

[Project README: E:\Work\Hub\GDCefGlue\README.md]
# GDCefGlue

A CEF (Chromium Embedded Framework) browser control for Godot 4.x using CefGlue.

[中文文档](README_CN.md)

## Features

- GPU hardware acceleration support
- IME support for Chinese/Japanese/Korean input
- Popup handling
- Complete keyboard and mouse support
- Easy integration with Godot 4.x

## Performance Demo

### WebGL Aquarium

Supports WebGL rendering with 20,000 fish at stable 120fps:

![WebGL Aquarium](img/WebGL水族馆.png)

## Quick Start

### C# (Plugin)

Add a `CefGlueControl` node to your scene:

```csharp
var browser = new CefGlueControl();
browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org";
AddChild(browser);
```

### GDScript (GDExtension)

```gdscript
var browser: CefGlueControl = $CefGlueControl
browser.InitialUrl = "https://godotengine.org"
browser.FrameRate = 120

# Connect to signals
browser.BrowserInitialized.connect(_on_ready)
browser.AddressChanged.connect(_on_address_changed)
browser.LoadStart.connect(_on_loading)
browser.LoadEnd.connect(_on_done)
browser.LoadError.connect(_on_error)
```

## Requirements

- **Godot Engine**: 4.6.0 or later (with .NET/Mono support)
- **.NET SDK**: 8.0 or later
- **Windows**: x64 architecture

## Dependency Options

### Option 1: NuGet Package (Recommended)

The easiest way to use GDCefGlue. All necessary files are automatically copied during build.

**CEF 149 (Recommended):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="149.7827.156" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="149.0.4" />
</ItemGroup>
```

**CEF 120 (Official, not recommended — too old):**
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <PackageReference Include="CefGlue.Common" Version="120.6099.0" />
  <PackageReference Include="chromiumembeddedframework.runtime.win-x64" Version="120.2.3" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### Option 2: Build from Source

If you need the latest version or want to customize CefGlue:

```bash
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git
```

Place the cloned repository in your project directory:
```
YourProject/
├── GDCefGlue/                    ← This repository
│   ├── plugin/                   ← Godot .NET project (addon source + demo)
│   │   └── addons/GCefGlue/     ←    CefGlueControl C# scripts
│   ├── extension/                ← GDExtension C# project (AOT native lib)
│   │   └── Dll/                  ←    godot-dotnet bindings
│   ├── test/GDExtensionGame/     ← Godot project for testing GDExtension
│   ├── Nuget/                    ← Local NuGet packages (LFS)
│   ├── img/
│   └── README*.md
└── CefGlue/                      ← Cloned CefGlue repository
```

## Project Types

### GDExtension Project (Experimental)

Located in `extension/` directory. For Godot 4.6+.

> **Warning:** This project is currently unstable and not recommended for production use.

**Features:**
- Uses Godot's GDExtension system
- Compiled as native AOT library
- Better performance and smaller file size
- Requires manual CEF file copying during export
- Entry point: `gdcefglue_library_init`

**Structure:**
- `extension/` - C# source code for GDExtension
- `extension/Dll/` - Godot .NET bindings (from [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet))
- `test/GDExtensionGame/` - Godot project for testing

**Build Instructions:**

1. **Get Godot .NET Bindings:**
   ```bash
   git clone https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet.git
   cd godot-dotnet
   dotnet build -p:GenerateGodotBindings=true
   ```
   Copy the generated `Godot.Bindings.dll` and related files to `extension/Dll/`.

2. **Build GDExtension:**
   ```bash
   cd extension
   dotnet publish -c Debug -r win-x64 --self-contained true
   ```

3. **Deploy:**
   Copy all files from `Debug/net10.0/win-x64/publish/` to `test/GDExtensionGame/lib/`.

4. **Run:**
   Open the project with Godot 4.6 and run.

### Normal C# Project

Located in `plugin/` directory. Traditional Godot C# project approach.

**Features:**
- Standard Godot .NET SDK project
- Uses `Godot.NET.Sdk`
- CEF files are automatically copied when using NuGet packages
- May need manual resource copying after export (except NuGet packages)
- Source code in `addons/GCefGlue/`

**When to use:**
- If you prefer traditional Godot C# development
- If GDExtension doesn't meet your needs

Update your `.csproj` to reference the projects:
```xml
<ItemGroup>
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue\CefGlue.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common\CefGlue.Common.csproj" />
  <ProjectReference Include="..\CefGlue\CefGlue.Common.Shared\CefGlue.Common.Shared.csproj" />
</ItemGroup>
```

### CefGlue Sources

| Source | CEF Version | Status | NuGet | GitHub |
|--------|-------------|--------|-------|--------|
| youfch/CefGlue | 149 | Maintained (Recommended) | `CefGlue.Common 149.7827.156` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue) |
| youfch/cef.redist.linux | 149 | Linux runtime | `cef.redist.linux64 149.0.4` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/cef.redist.linux) |
| youfch/cef.redist.osx | 149 | macOS runtime | `cef.redist.osx64 149.0.4` | [GitHub](https://github.com/youfch/cef.redist.osx) |
| OutSystems/CefGlue | 120 | Official (Legacy) | `CefGlue.Common 120.6099.0` | [GitHub](https://github.com/OutSystems/CefGlue) |

## Build Instructions

### Using NuGet Package

```powershell
# Restore packages
dotnet restore

# Build
dotnet build
```

### Using Source Code

```powershell
# Clone CefGlue
git clone https://github.com/youfch/CefGlue.git

# Build
dotnet restore
dotnet build
```

## Build Output

After a successful build, the following files will be generated:

**Core Files:**
- `GDCefGlue.dll` - Main plugin assembly
- `Xilium.CefGlue.dll` - CefGlue wrapper
- `Xilium.CefGlue.Common.dll` - Common functionality

**CEF Native Files:**
- `libcef.dll` - Chromium core library
- `chrome_*.pak` - UI resources
- `resources.pak` - Application resources
- `locales\*.pak` - Language packs

**BrowserProcess Files:**
- `CefGlueBrowserProcess\` - Browser subprocess files

## Export for Distribution

### Using NuGet Package

When using NuGet packages, all necessary files are automatically copied during build. Just export your Godot project normally.

### Using Source Code

When using source code dependencies, you need to manually copy files after export. See [CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md](CEF_EXPORT_GUIDE.md) for details.

## CefGlueControl Properties

| Property | Type | Default | Description |
|----------|------|---------|-------------|
| `InitialUrl` | string | "about:blank" | The URL to load when the browser is created |
| `OpenPopupInCurrentBrowser` | bool | true | If true, popup windows navigate in the current browser |
| `GpuAcceleration` | bool | true | If true, enables GPU hardware acceleration |
| `FrameRate` | int | 60 | Browser frame rate, range 1-360 |
| `Transparent` | bool | false | If true, enables transparent background support |

### Static Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `UseGpuAcceleration` | bool | Global GPU acceleration setting, must be set before CEF initialization |
| `UseTransparent` | bool | Global transparent background setting, must be set before CEF initialization |

### Read-only Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `Address` | string | Current page URL |
| `IsBrowserInitialized` | bool | Whether the browser is initialized |
| `IsLoading` | bool | Whether the page is loading |
| `Title` | string | Current page title |

## CefGlueControl Methods

| Method | Description |
|--------|-------------|
| `GoBack()` | Navigate back in history |
| `GoForward()` | Navigate forward in history |
| `NavigateToUrl(string url)` | Navigate to the specified URL |
| `Reload(bool ignoreCache = false)` | Reload the current page, optionally ignoring cache |
| `ExecuteJavaScript(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript code |
| `EvaluateJavaScript<T>(string code, ...)` | Execute JavaScript and return result |
| `ShowDeveloperTools()` | Open developer tools |
| `CloseDeveloperTools()` | Close developer tools |

## JS ↔ C# Bridge

GDCefGlue provides bidirectional communication between JavaScript and C# via the `godot://` protocol.

### JS → C#

JavaScript sends messages by navigating to a `godot://bridge` URL:

```javascript
// Using iframe (no _godotBridge needed)
var i = document.createElement('iframe');
i.src = 'godot://bridge?type=ping&cb=myCallbackId&payload=' + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify({}));
document.body.appendChild(i);
```

Or with the `_godotBridge` helper (inject in your HTML):

```javascript
window._godotBridge = {
    _onResponse: function(cbId, msg) { /* resolve Promise by cbId */ },
    _onMessage: function(msg) { /* handle C# push messages */ },
    sendToGodot: function(data) {
        var i = document.createElement('iframe');
        i.src = 'godot://bridge?type=' + data.type
              + '&cb=' + data.cbId
              + '&payload=' + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(data.payload));
        document.body.appendChild(i);
    }
};
```

### C# → JS

```csharp
// Push a message to JS (calls window._godotBridge._onMessage)
browser.SendToJs("{\"type\":\"update\",\"payload\":{\"count\":42}}");

// Reply to a specific JS request (calls window._godotBridge._onResponse)
browser.SendResponse("myCallbackId", "{\"status\":\"ok\"}");
```

### C# Event

```csharp
browser.BridgeRequest += (type, payload, cbId) => {
    switch (type) {
        case "ping":
            browser.SendResponse(cbId, "{\"status\":\"pong\"}");
            break;
    }
};
```

| API | Direction | Description |
|-----|-----------|-------------|
| `BridgeRequest` event | JS → C# | Fired when JS calls `godot://bridge` |
| `SendToJs(json)` | C# → JS | Push message to `window._godotBridge._onMessage` |
| `SendResponse(cbId, json)` | C# → JS | Reply to a specific JS callback via `window._godotBridge._onResponse` |

## CefGlueControl Signals

| Signal | Parameters | Description |
|--------|-----------|-------------|
| `BrowserInitialized` | — | Emitted when the browser is fully initialized and ready |
| `AddressChanged` | `url: string` | Emitted when the current page URL changes |
| `TitleChanged` | `title: string` | Emitted when the page title changes |
| `LoadStart` | — | Emitted when a page starts loading |
| `LoadEnd` | — | Emitted when a page finishes loading |
| `LoadError` | `errorText: string, failedUrl: string` | Emitted when a page fails to load |

## GPU Configuration

```csharp
// Enable GPU acceleration (default) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = true;

// Disable GPU acceleration (software rendering) - set in inspector
// GpuAcceleration = false;
```

**Note:** This property is exposed to the Godot inspector. Set it before the control is initialized.

## Troubleshooting

### GPU Process Crashed

1. Ensure all CEF files are copied correctly
2. Try disabling GPU acceleration in the inspector

### Missing DLLs

1. Run `dotnet restore` to restore NuGet packages
2. Clean and rebuild the solution

### Blank Page

1. Check if `locales` directory exists
2. Ensure `resources.pak` is present

## Known Issues

1. **Right-click Context Menu**: Not supported
2. **Network Notification**: `WSALookupServiceBegin failed with: 10108` is a normal warning

GDCefGlue is licensed under the MIT License. See [LICENSE](LICENSE) for details.

- [Godot Engine](https://godotengine.org) - MIT
- [godot-dotnet](https://github.com/raulsntos/godot-dotnet) - MIT

44:     /// <summary>
45:     /// JS → C# bridge handler.
46:     /// Test in browser console:
47:     ///   fetch('godot://bridge?type=ping&cb=test1&payload={}').catch(()=>{})
48:     ///   var i=document.createElement('iframe'); i.src='godot://bridge?type=ping&cb=test1&payload=%7B%7D'; document.body.appendChild(i);
49:     /// Or if _godotBridge is injected:
50:     ///   window._godotBridge.sendToGodot({type:'ping', payload:{}})
51:     /// </summary>
52:     private void OnBridgeRequest(string type, string payload, string cbId)
53:     {
54:         GD.Print($"[Bridge] type={type}, cb={cbId ?? "none"}, payload={payload}");
55: 
56:         switch (type)
57:         {
58:             case "ping":
59:                 _browser.SendResponse(cbId, "{\"status\":\"pong\"}");
60:                 CallDeferred(nameof(UpdateStatusLabel), "Bridge: ping → pong");
61:                 break;
62: 
63:             case "status":
64:                 var status = $"{{\"initialized\":{_browser.IsBrowserInitialized.ToString().ToLower()},\"loading\":{_browser.IsLoading.ToString().ToLower()},\"title\":\"{_browser.Title}\"}}";
65:                 _browser.SendResponse(cbId, status);
66:                 break;
67: 
68:             case "navigate":
69:                 var json = Json.ParseString(payload);
70:                 if (json.VariantType == Variant.Type.Dictionary)
71:                 {
72:                     var dict = json.AsGodotDictionary();
73:                     string url = dict.ContainsKey("url") ? dict["url"].AsString() : "";
74:                     if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(url))
75:                     {
76:                         CallDeferred(nameof(NavigateFromBridge), url);
77:                         _browser.SendResponse(cbId, "{\"status\":\"navigating\"}");
78:                     }
79:                     else
80:                     {
81:                         _browser.SendResponse(cbId, "{\"error\":\"url is empty\"}");
82:                     }
83:                 }
84:                 break;
85: 
86:             default:
87:                 GD.PrintErr($"[Bridge] Unknown type: {type}");
88:                 _browser.SendResponse(cbId, "{\"error\":\"unknown type\"}");
89:                 break;
90:         }
91:     }
92: 
93:     private void NavigateFromBridge(string url)
94:     {
95:         if (_browser != null)
96:         {
97:             _browser.Address = url;
98:             UpdateStatusLabel($"Bridge navigate: {url}");
99:         }
100:     }
101: 
102:     private void OnBrowserInitialized()
103:     {
104:         CallDeferred(nameof(UpdateStatusLabel), "Ready");
105:     }
106: 
107:     private void OnAddressChanged(object sender, string url)
108:     {
109:         CallDeferred(nameof(UpdateUrlInput), url);
110:     }
111: 
112:     private void OnLoadStart(object sender, Xilium.CefGlue.Common.Events.LoadStartEventArgs e)
113:     {
114:         CallDeferred(nameof(UpdateStatusLabel), "Loading...");
115:     }
116: 
117:     private void OnLoadEnd(object sender, Xilium.CefGlue.Common.Events.LoadEndEventArgs e)
118:     {
119:         CallDeferred(nameof(UpdateStatusLabel), "Done");
120:     }
121: 
122:     private void OnLoadError(object sender, Xilium.CefGlue.Common.Events.LoadErrorEventArgs e)
123:     {
124:         CallDeferred(nameof(UpdateStatusLabel), $"Error: {e.ErrorText}");
125:     }
126: 
127:     private void UpdateStatusLabel(string text)
128:     {
129:         if (_statusLabel != null)
130:             _statusLabel.Text = text;
131:     }
132: 
133:     private void UpdateUrlInput(string url)
134:     {
135:         if (_urlInput != null)
136:             _urlInput.Text = url;
137:     }
138: 
139:     private void OnBackPressed()
140:     {
141:         _browser?.GoBack();
142:     }
143: 
144:     private void OnForwardPressed()
145:     {
146:         _browser?.GoForward();
147:     }
148: 
149:     private void OnReloadPressed()
150:     {
151:         _browser?.Reload();
152:     }
153: 
154:     private void OnOpenDevPressed()
155:     {
156:         _browser?.ShowDeveloperTools();
157:     }
158: 
159:     private void OnGoPressed()
160:     {
161:         NavigateToUrl();
162:     }
163: 
164:     private void OnUrlSubmitted(string text)
165:     {
166:         NavigateToUrl();
167:     }
168: 
169:     private void NavigateToUrl()
170:     {
171:         var url = _urlInput?.Text?.Trim();
172:         if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(url))
173:             return;
174: 
175:         if (!url.StartsWith("http://") && !url.StartsWith("https://") && !url.StartsWith("about:") && !url.StartsWith("file://"))
176:         {
177:             url = "https://" + url;
178:         }
179: 
180:         if (_browser != null)
181:         {
182:             _browser.Address = url;
183:         }
184:     }
185: }

8:     public partial class CefGlueControl
9:     {
10:         /// <summary>
11:         /// 嵌入窗口模式每帧处理：同步 CEF 子窗口位置/大小，跳过 OSR 纹理更新。
12:         /// 参考 godot_wry 的做法：检测 GlobalPosition / Size / WindowPosition / ContentScale 变化后
13:         /// 才调用 SetWindowPos，避免不必要的帧率开销。
14:         /// </summary>
15:         private void ProcessEmbeddedMode(double delta)
16:         {
17:             if (_browserHost == null || _godotHwnd == IntPtr.Zero)
18:                 return;
19: 
20:             var globalPos = GlobalPosition;
21:             var size = Size;
22:             if (size.X <= 0 || size.Y <= 0)
23:                 return;
24: 
25:             // 从 DisplayServer 获取内容缩放比（物理像素 / 虚拟像素）
26:             // 等价于 godot_wry 的 screen_get_content_scale_ex
27:             float contentScale = DisplayServer.ScreenGetScale();
28: 
29:             // 获取 Godot 窗口在屏幕上的位置（用于检测窗口移动）
30:             var windowPos = DisplayServer.WindowGetPosition();
31: 
32:             // 仅当有任何变化时才触发 SetWindowPos
33:             if (globalPos == _previousGlobalPos
34:                 && size == _previousSize
35:                 && windowPos == _previousWindowPos
36:                 && Math.Abs(contentScale - _previousContentScale) < 0.001f
37:                 && _cefChildHwnd != IntPtr.Zero
38:                 && size.X == _controlWidth && size.Y == _controlHeight)
39:             {
40:                 return; // 无变化，跳过
41:             }
42: 
43:             _previousGlobalPos = globalPos;
44:             _previousSize = size;
45:             _previousWindowPos = windowPos;
46:             _previousContentScale = contentScale;
47: 
48:             // 计算物理像素坐标
49:             int physX = (int)(globalPos.X * contentScale);
50:             int physY = (int)(globalPos.Y * contentScale);
51:             int physW = (int)(size.X * contentScale);
52:             int physH = (int)(size.Y * contentScale);
53: 
54:             if (_cefChildHwnd == IntPtr.Zero)
55:             {
56:                 // GetWindowHandle 可能还没准备好（异步创建），重试
57:                 _cefChildHwnd = _browserHost.GetWindowHandle();
58:                 if (_cefChildHwnd == IntPtr.Zero)
59:                     return;
60: 
61:                 GD.Print($"CefGlueControl: CEF child HWND acquired = 0x{_cefChildHwnd.ToInt64():X8}");
62:             }
63: 
64:             // 同步 CEF 子窗口位置和大小（坐标相对于父窗口客户区）
65:             NativeWindowMethods.MovePlatformWindow(
66:                 _cefChildHwnd, physX, physY, physW, physH);
67: 
68:             // 通知 CEF 窗口大小变化
69:             if (physW != _controlWidth || physH != _controlHeight)
70:             {
71:                 _controlWidth = physW;
72:                 _controlHeight = physH;
73:                 _browserHost.WasResized();

81:             }

## 完整理解

### 你要做的是什么

用 **C#** 在 Godot 中创建一个 **系统 WebView 控件**，替换掉 rust 版的 `godot_wry`。

### 三个参考源的关键信息

| 参考源 | 技术栈 | 核心模式 | 可借鉴的部分 |
|--------|--------|----------|-------------|
| **godot_wry** | Rust + WRY | 系统浏览器（Win: WebView2, Mac: WebKit, Linux: WebKitGTK） | 窗口嵌入逻辑、输入事件转发（JS注入）、`res://` 协议、IPC 机制 |
| **GDCefGlue** | C# + CEF (Chromium) | 全功能 C# Godot 插件，OSR + 嵌入窗口双模式 | C# 项目结构、`NativeWindowMethods` 跨平台窗口管理、`ProcessEmbeddedMode` 位置同步 |
| **604Assembly** | C# + WebView2 | i3D 引擎中直接嵌入 WebView2 | WebView2 初始化、`SetParent` 嵌入、`SetWindowPos` 同步 |

### 架构方案

```
GodotWebViewSharp/
├── src/                          ← Godot .NET 项目
│   ├── project.godot
│   ├── GodotWebViewSharp.csproj
│   ├── addons/
│   │   └── WebViewSharp/         ← 插件源码
│   │       ├── WebViewControl.cs              主类 + 属性导出
│   │       ├── WebViewControl.Window.cs       窗口嵌入（参考 godot_wry + GDCefGlue）
│   │       ├── WebViewControl.Input.cs        输入事件转发（参考 godot_wry JS注入）
│   │       ├── WebViewControl.IPC.cs           JS ↔ C# 桥接
│   │       └── WebViewControl.Navigation.cs    导航 API
│   ├── Demo/
│   │   ├── DemoBrowser.cs
│   │   ├── DemoBrowser.tscn
│   │   └── html/                              demo 用 HTML
│   └── ...
└── ...
```

**关键设计决策：**

1. **Windows 平台优先**：使用 `Microsoft.Web.WebView2`（系统 WebView2，跟 godot_wry 用 WRY 底层一样）
2. **窗口嵌入**：`SetParent` 将 WebView2 嵌入 Godot 窗口 → 每帧 `SetWindowPos` 同步位置（参考 GDCefGlue 的 `ProcessEmbeddedMode`）
3. **输入转发**：注入 JS 脚本捕获鼠标/键盘事件 → 通过 WebView2 的 `WebMessageReceived` 接收 → 构造 Godot `InputEvent` 推送（参考 godot_wry 的 IPC 模式）
4. **JS ↔ C# Bridge**：双向通信，参考 godot_wry 的 `ipc_message` signal + GDCefGlue 的 `BridgeRequest` 事件
5. **`res://` 协议**：通过拦截导航或自定义 scheme 支持
6. **跨平台扩展**：`NativeWindowMethods` 模式预留 macOS/Linux 接口

Title: NativeWebView | Avalonia Docs
URL: https://docs.avaloniaui.net/controls/web/nativewebview
Published: 2026-05-14T10:04:04.000Z
Author: N/A
Highlights:
`NativeWebView` is a control that provides a native web browser implementation for Avalonia and WPF applications. It wraps platform-specific web controls and provides a unified API for web browsing functionality.
...
## Platform support​
...
| Feature | Windows WebView2-Edge | macOS/iOS WKWebView | Linux WPE WebKit | Android | Browser |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `NativeWebView` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✗* |
| `TryGetCommandManager` | ✓ | ✓ | ✗* | ✓ | ✗* |
| `TryGetCookieManager` | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✗* |
| `ShowPrintUI` | ✓ | ✓ | ✗* | ✗* | ✗* |
| `PrintToPdfStreamAsync` | ✓ | ✓** | ✗* | ✗* | ✗* |
...
On Linux, `NativeWebView` is rendered through WPE WebKit using offscreen (SHM) rendering. Install the `libwpewebkit-2.0`, `libwpe-1.0`, and `libWPEBackend-fdo-1.0` runtime libraries — see the Linux prerequisites.
...
If WPE is unavailable, you can opt in to the WebKitGTK adapter by setting `LinuxWpeWebViewEnvironmentRequestedEventArgs.PreferWebKitGtkInstead` to `true`. Alternatively, use `NativeWebDialog` instead.

Native webview stack for Avalonia that stays on top of platform-native engines instead of bundling Chromium.
...

...
| Package | NuGet | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `NativeWebView` | Avalonia control facade API. |
| `NativeWebView.Core` | Shared contracts, controllers, feature model, and backend factory. |
| `NativeWebView.Dialog` | Dialog facade API. |
| `NativeWebView.Auth` | Web authentication broker facade API. |
| `NativeWebView.Interop` | Native handle contracts and structs. |
| `NativeWebView.Platform.Windows` | Windows backend registration and implementation. |
| `NativeWebView.Platform.macOS` | macOS backend registration and implementation. |
| `NativeWebView.Platform.Linux` | Linux backend registration and implementation. |
| `NativeWebView.Platform.iOS` | iOS backend registration and implementation. |
| `NativeWebView.Platform.Android` | Android backend registration and implementation. |
| `NativeWebView.Platform.Browser` | Browser backend registration and implementation. |
...
- `NativeWebView` control for embedded native browser surfaces inside Avalonia.
- `NativeWebDialog` facade for dialog and popup browser workflows.
- `WebAuthenticationBroker` facade for OAuth and interactive sign-in.
- Platform backends for Windows, macOS, Linux, iOS, Android, and Browser.
- Optional airspace-mitigation modes:`Embedded`,`GpuSurface`, and`Offscreen`.
- Diagnostics, capability reporting, render-frame export, integrity metadata, and smoke-testable sample apps.
...
| Platform | `NativeWebView` control | `NativeWebDialog` | `WebAuthenticationBroker` | Per-instance proxy |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Windows | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented |
| macOS | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented on macOS 14+ |
| Linux | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented |
| iOS | Implemented when built with the .NET 8 Apple workload | Unsupported | Implemented when built with the .NET 8 Apple workload | Implemented on iOS 17+ when built with the .NET 8 Apple workload |
| Android | Implemented when built with the .NET 8 Android workload | Unsupported | Implemented when built with the .NET 8 Android workload | Contract-only, app-wide platform API only |
| Browser | Implemented when built for the browser target | Unsupported | Implemented when built for the browser target | Unsupported |
...
```
dotnet add package NativeWebView
dotnet add package NativeWebView.Platform.Windows
...
Swap`NativeWebView.Platform.Windows` for`NativeWebView.Platform.macOS`,`NativeWebView.Platform.Linux`,`NativeWebView.Platform.iOS`,`NativeWebView.Platform.Android`, or`NativeWebView.Platform.Browser` as needed.
...
Use`NativeWebDialog` when the browser should live in a separate desktop window, and`WebAuthenticationBroker` when the main goal is an auth callback flow rather than general browsing.`NativeWebDialog` is intentionally desktop-only in the current repo runtime;`WebAuthenticationBroker` is implemented on Windows, macOS, Linux, iOS, Android, and Browser with the platform-specific limitations documented below.
...
Current embedded runtime implementation exists on Windows, macOS, and Linux in the default desktop build. iOS and Android runtime support is compiled from their platform-targeted backend assemblies rather than the default`net10.0` contract build, and the Browser runtime is compiled from the browser-targeted backend assembly. Windows and Linux now also ship real`NativeWebDialog` runtimes backed by native desktop windows plus their existing WebView2/WebKitGTK engines, and`WebAuthenticationBroker` runs real modal or popup browser sessions whenever the corresponding platform runtime assembly is present. The Browser implementation hosts a real embedded`iframe` through Avalonia Browser native hosting and a DOM bridge; navigation is real, but script execution,`window.chrome.webview` emulation, and new-window interception depend on same-origin access or explicit`postMessage` cooperation from the hosted page, and normal browser frame restrictions such as`X-Frame-Options`/`Content-Security-Policy: frame-ancestors` still apply. Browser authentication uses`window.open`, so popup support plus an inspectable`http` or`https` callback URL are required there; custom-scheme callbacks and`UseHttpPost` are not supported on the current browser runtime path. The iOS implementation hosts`WKWebView` through a backend-owned`UIView` attachment path and a modal authentication controller when`NativeWebView.Platform.iOS` is built with the .NET 10 Apple workload;`NativeWebDialog` remains unsupported there. The Android implementation hosts`android.webkit.WebView` through a backend-owned child`View` attachment and a dedicated authentication activity when`NativeWebView.Platform.Android` is built with the .NET 10 Android workload;`NativeWebDialog` remains unsupported there. Per-instance proxy application is effective on the Windows and Linux`NativeWebView` and`NativeWebDialog` runtime paths, on macOS 14+ for`NativeWebView` and`NativeWebDialog`, and on iOS 17+ for the embedded`NativeWebView` control when the iOS runtime assembly is present. The macOS and iOS implementations support explicit`http`,`https`, and`socks5` proxy servers, credentials, and bypass domains, and use a dedicated`WKWebsiteDataStore` identity derived from the instance configuration so proxied views do not fall back to private-browsing storage semantics. On Linux, explicit`http`,`https`, and`socks` proxies are runtime-applied on the X11 control/dialog paths;`AutoConfigUrl`, embedded proxy credentials, and direct PDF export are not currently applied there. On Android, the official AndroidX proxy override remains app-wide, so per-instance proxy configuration is still contract-only on the current runtime path. Browser targets remain unsupported for per-instance proxy application because the host browser does not expose per-instance engine proxy control.`WebAuthenticationBroker.UseHttpPost` currently remains unsupported on all runtime backends in this repo.
...
Use`NativeWebViewPlatformImplementationStatusMatrix.Get(platform)` to inspect the honest current repo runtime status for each target. Use`NativeWebViewProxyPlatformSupportMatrix.Get(platform)` for proxy-specific status. The current core package also exposes`NativeWebViewWindowsProxyArgumentsBuilder` and`NativeWebViewLinuxProxySettingsBuilder` so future or custom backends can translate shared proxy options into WebView2/WebKitGTK-specific configuration payloads without duplicating parsing logic. Exact`UserDataFolder`/`CacheFolder`/`CookieDataFolder`/`SessionDataFolder` behavior remains backend-specific. In the current repo, Linux currently runtime-applies`CookieDataFolder`,`Language`,`IsInPrivateModeEnabled`, and explicit proxy settings on the embedded control path, while`UserDataFolder`,`CacheFolder`, and`SessionDataFolder` remain backend-specific configuration contracts there. The macOS and iOS`WKWebView` proxy/runtime paths use storage-path values as part of isolated data-store identity rather than direct physical directory mapping.

Title: Add desktop download API and stabilize Linux embedding
URL: https://github.com/wieslawsoltes/NativeWebView/pull/17
Published: N/A
Author: N/A
Highlights:
merged
- Author: StefanKoell
- Created:
...
2026-0
...
T14:17:34Z
- Updated: 2026-06-28T08:41:10Z
- Repository: wieslawsoltes/NativeWebView
- Number: #17
- +5543 -301 in 27 files
- Merged: 2026-06-28T08:41:10Z
- Merge commit: add0a27bbac1dd20da011115b6eba07f566f
...
## Summary 
 
Adds a cross-platform desktop download API for NativeWebView and wires it into the Windows, macOS, and Linux backends. 
 
This introduces a shared download manager abstraction with lifecycle events, destination selection, progress snapshots, 
cancellation, and platform-specific pause/resume capabilities where available. The branch also stabilizes the Linux 
WebKitGTK/X11 embedded control path so downloads can be tested through the normal embedded sample experience. 
 
 ## Highlights 
 
 - Add the core download API: 
 - download manager abstraction 
 - download item snapshots 
 - queued lifecycle state 
 - destination-selection deferrals 
 - started/changed/completed events 
 - cancel, pause, and resume actions with capability reporting 
 
 - Expose download support through: 
 - platform feature flags 
 - `TryGetDownloadManager` 
 - NativeWebView controls 
 - dialogs 
 - controllers 
 - backend abstractions 
 
 - Implement Windows WebView2 downloads: 
 - native destination selection 
 - progress and state updates 
 - completion/failure mapping 
 - cancel/pause/resume when supported by WebView2 
 
 - Implement macOS WKWebView downloads: 
 - `WKDownload` integration 
 - navigation/action/response download routing 
 - destination selection 
 - progress, completion, cancellation, pause, and resume where supported 
 - context-menu “Download Linked File” support 
 - related macOS WebKit improvements for new-window forwarding, focus bridging, and favicon preservation 
 
 - Implement Linux WebKitGTK downloads: 
 - route WebKit download-started events through the managed download manager 
 - support direct links, context-menu downloads, unsupported response downloads, and programmatic downloads 
 - use managed HTTP/file transfer for the Linux download path 
 - avoid broad URL heuristics and only intercept high-confidence binary/archive download URLs 
 - avoid creating destination files until HTTP responses succeed 
 - clean up incomplete files after failed transfers 
 
 - Stabilize Linux embedded WebKitGTK/X11 hosting: 
 - add native GTK event-loop pumping 
 - add native bounds synchronization 
 - use GTKPlug/XEmbed for X11 embedding 
 - harden widget sizing, realization, expansion, and background color handling 
 - avoid setting null WebKitGTK user agents 
 - stop mutating `WAYLAND_DISPLAY` 
 
 - Update sample, docs, and tests: 
 - add a local download test server for the desktop sample 
 - expose download testing through the sample UI 
 - update platform support docs and changelog fragment 
 - add focused download manager tests 
 - update render/frame tests for the current Linux behavior 
 
 ## Notes 
 
 The desktop download API is now implemented across Windows, macOS, and Linux with platform-specific behavior exposed 
 through action capabilities. Linux currently uses the working WebKitGTK/X11 embedded path and managed transfer fallback; 
 WPE/offscreen support remains intentionally disabled until a complete renderer implementation is available. 

## Timeline

| Platform | `NativeWebView` control | `NativeWebDialog` | `WebAuthenticationBroker` | Per-instance proxy |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Windows | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented |
| macOS | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented on macOS 14+ |
| Linux | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented |
| iOS | Implemented when built with the .NET 8 Apple workload | Unsupported | Implemented when built with the .NET 8 Apple workload | Implemented on iOS 17+ when built with the .NET 8 Apple workload |
| Android | Implemented when built with the .NET 8 Android workload | Unsupported | Implemented when built with the .NET 8 Android workload | Contract-only, app-wide platform API only |
| Browser | Implemented when built for the browser target | Unsupported | Implemented when built for the browser target | Unsupported |
...
Windows, macOS, and Linux
-
...
currently supported runtime
...
Swap`NativeWebView.Platform.Windows` for`NativeWebView.Platform.macOS`,`NativeWebView.Platform.Linux`,`NativeWebView.Platform.iOS`,`NativeWebView.Platform.Android`, or`NativeWebView.Platform.Browser` as needed.
...
Use`NativeWebDialog` when the browser should live in a separate desktop window, and`WebAuthenticationBroker` when the main goal is an auth callback flow rather than general browsing.`NativeWebDialog` is intentionally desktop-only in the current repo runtime;`WebAuthenticationBroker` is implemented on Windows, macOS, Linux, iOS, Android, and Browser with the platform-specific limitations documented below.
...
Current embedded runtime implementation exists on Windows, macOS, and Linux in the default desktop build. iOS and Android runtime support is compiled from their platform-targeted backend assemblies rather than the default`net10.0` contract build, and the Browser runtime is compiled from the browser-targeted backend assembly. Windows and Linux now also ship real`NativeWebDialog` runtimes backed by native desktop windows plus their existing WebView2/WebKitGTK engines, and`WebAuthenticationBroker` runs real modal or popup browser sessions whenever the corresponding platform runtime assembly is present. The Browser implementation hosts a real embedded`iframe` through Avalonia Browser native hosting and a DOM bridge; navigation is real, but script execution,`window.chrome.webview` emulation, and new-window interception depend on same-origin access or explicit`postMessage` cooperation from the hosted page, and normal browser frame restrictions such as`X-Frame-Options`/`Content-Security-Policy: frame-ancestors` still apply. Browser authentication uses`window.open`, so popup support plus an inspectable`http` or`https` callback URL are required there; custom-scheme callbacks and`UseHttpPost` are not supported on the current browser runtime path. The iOS implementation hosts`WKWebView` through a backend-owned`UIView` attachment path and a modal authentication controller when`NativeWebView.Platform.iOS` is built with the .NET 10 Apple workload;`NativeWebDialog` remains unsupported there. The Android implementation hosts`android.webkit.WebView` through a backend-owned child`View` attachment and a dedicated authentication activity when`NativeWebView.Platform.Android` is built with the .NET 10 Android workload;`NativeWebDialog` remains unsupported there. Per-instance proxy application is effective on the Windows and Linux`NativeWebView` and`NativeWebDialog` runtime paths, on macOS 14+ for`NativeWebView` and`NativeWebDialog`, and on iOS 17+ for the embedded`NativeWebView` control when the iOS runtime assembly is present. The macOS and iOS implementations support explicit`http`,`https`, and`socks5` proxy servers, credentials, and bypass domains, and use a dedicated`WKWebsiteDataStore` identity derived from the instance configuration so proxied views do not fall back to private-browsing storage semantics. On Linux, explicit`http`,`https`, and`socks` proxies are runtime-applied on the X11 control/dialog paths;`AutoConfigUrl`, embedded proxy credentials, and direct PDF export are not currently applied there. On Android, the official AndroidX proxy override remains app-wide, so per-instance proxy configuration is still contract-only on the current runtime path. Browser targets remain unsupported for per-instance proxy application because the host browser does not expose per-instance engine proxy control.`WebAuthenticationBroker.UseHttpPost` currently remains unsupported on all runtime backends in this repo.
...
Use`NativeWebViewPlatformImplementationStatusMatrix.Get(platform)` to inspect the honest current repo runtime status for each target. Use`NativeWebViewProxyPlatformSupportMatrix.Get(platform)` for proxy-specific status. The current core package also exposes`NativeWebViewWindowsProxyArgumentsBuilder` and`NativeWebViewLinuxProxySettingsBuilder` so future or custom backends can translate shared proxy options into WebView2/WebKitGTK-specific configuration payloads without duplicating parsing logic. Exact`UserDataFolder`/`CacheFolder`/`CookieDataFolder`/`SessionDataFolder` behavior remains backend-specific. In the current repo, Linux currently runtime-applies`CookieDataFolder`,`Language`,`IsInPrivateModeEnabled`, and explicit proxy settings on the embedded control path, while`UserDataFolder`,`CacheFolder`, and`SessionDataFolder` remain backend-specific configuration contracts there. The macOS and iOS`WKWebView` proxy/runtime paths use storage-path values as part of isolated data-store identity rather than direct physical directory mapping.
...
- Hosted docs: wieslawsoltes.github.io/NativeWebView
- Getting started: Quickstart
- Control surface: NativeWebView
- Desktop dialog surface: NativeWebDialog
- Auth surface: WebAuthenticationBroker
- Rendering and interop: Render Modes
- Platform notes: Platforms
- Diagnostics and operations: Diagnostics
- API reference: wieslawsoltes.github.io/NativeWebView/api

Now I have a clear picture of NativeWebView. It's an Avalonia control but the important thing is that it wraps platform-native webview engines:

- Windows: WebView2 (Edge Chromium)
- macOS: WKWebView (Safari)
- Linux: WebKitGTK / WPE WebKit

Plus it supports multiple render modes:
- **Embedded** - native window child (default, what we want)
- **GpuSurface** - GPU surface sharing
- **Offscreen** - offscreen rendering (OSR)

The user specifically said:
1. Windows WebView2 doesn't support OSR (that's correct - WebView2 doesn't have true offscreen rendering like CEF does)
2. But other platforms might support OSR
3. The goal is to match godot_wry

Now, the key insight is: NativeWebView is an **Avalonia** control, which means it depends on Avalonia UI framework. We can't directly use it in Godot.

However, we can:
1. **Reference the patterns** from NativeWebView (how they do platform-specific webview wrapping)
2. **NOT use the Avalonia package** directly in Godot
3. Instead, write our own Godot C# WebView control that wraps platform webviews directly

The approach should be:
1. **Windows**: Use `Microsoft.Web.WebView2.Core` directly (this is what WRY uses too)
2. **macOS**: Use native interop with WKWebView (or use WebView2 if available)
3. **Linux**: Use WebKitGTK interop

Since we're building a Godot plugin (not an Avalonia app), we need to write the WebView control from scratch as a custom Godot `Control` node, using platform-specific APIs.

Let me now think about the full architecture and start creating the project.

**Project Structure:**
```
src/                           ← Godot .NET project
├── project.godot
├── GodotWebViewSharp.csproj
├── addons/
│   └── WebViewSharp/
│       ├── WebViewControl.cs              Main class
│       ├── WebViewControl.Windows.cs      Windows WebView2 implementation
│       ├── WebViewControl.Input.cs        Input event forwarding (JS injection)
│       ├── WebViewControl.IPC.cs          JS ↔ C# bridge
│       └── Platform/
│           ├── NativeWindowMethods.cs     Cross-platform window management
│           └── WindowsWebView.cs          Windows WebView2 wrapper
├── Demo/
│   ├── DemoBrowser.cs
│   ├── DemoBrowser.tscn
│   └── html/
│       └── index.html
└── icon.svg
```

Let me start implementing this. The user wants to get this done.

Actually, let me think about this more carefully. The key challenge with WebView2 in Godot:

1. **Window embedding**: WebView2 creates a native HWND child window. We need to:
   - Get the Godot window's HWND via `DisplayServer.WindowGetNativeHandle`
   - Create WebView2 controller with that parent window
   - Use `SetWindowPos` every frame to sync position/size (like GDCefGlue's `ProcessEmbeddedMode`)

2. **Input forwarding**: Since WebView2 is a child HWND, it captures all mouse/keyboard input directly. This is actually GOOD for normal usage. But godot_wry also has a `forward_input_events` mode where it captures events and forwards them through Godot's input system (for things like drag-and-drop, or when full_window_size=true).

3. **JS ↔ C# IPC**: WebView2 has built-in `CoreWebView2.PostWebMessageAsJson` and `WebMessageReceived` event.

4. **res:// protocol**: We can intercept navigation and redirect `res://` URLs to local files, or use WebView2's `AddWebResourceRequestedFilter` to intercept.

Let me now implement this. I'll create the project structure first, then implement the core files.

The user also mentioned "对标 godot_wry" - let me check what features godot_wry has:

From the WebView struct:
- `url`, `html` - load URL or HTML
- `full_window_size` - full screen or custom position
- `transparent` - transparent background
- `background_color` - background color
- `devtools` - enable devtools
- `headers` - custom HTTP headers
- `user_agent` - custom user agent
- `zoom_hotkeys` - zoom with Ctrl+/- 
- `clipboard` - clipboard access
- `incognito` - incognito mode
- `focused_when_created` - auto focus
- `forward_input_events` - forward events to Godot
- `autoplay` - autoplay media

Methods:
- `post_message(string)` - Send message to JS
- `eval(string)` - Execute JS
- `load_url(string)` - Load URL
- `load_html(string)` - Load HTML
- `resize()` - Resize webview
- `update_visibility()` - Update visibility
- `set_visible(bool)` - Set visibility
- `clear_all_browsing_data()` - Clear cache
- `close_devtools()` / `open_devtools()` / `is_devtools_open()` - DevTools
- `focus()` / `focus_parent()` - Focus control
- `print()` - Print page
- `reload()` - Reload
- `zoom(double)` - Zoom
- `reparent_webview(new_window_id)` - Reparent to another window

Signals:
- `ipc_message(string)` - From JS
- `page_load_started(string)` - Load started
- `page_load_finished(string)` - Load finished

OK, let me start implementing.

For WebView2, the key patterns from the reference implementations:

**godot_wry (Rust):**
- Get native window handle via DisplayServer
- Create webview as child of the Godot window
- Every frame, check position/size changes and update bounds
- Inject JS to forward mouse/keyboard events
- IPC via `window.ipc.postMessage`

**GDCefGlue (C#):**
- `ProcessEmbeddedMode` - delta-check position/size change
- `NativeWindowMethods.MovePlatformWindow` - cross-platform SetWindowPos
- `GetWindowHandle()` - get CEF child HWND

**604Assembly (C#):**
- `CoreWebView2Controller` with `Bounds` property
- `SetParent` to embed as child
- `FindWindowEx("Chrome_WidgetWin_0")` to get WebView2 HWND

For my implementation, I'll use `Microsoft.Web.WebView2.Core` NuGet package which provides the managed WebView2 API.

Key architectural decisions:
1. **EmbeddedWindow mode** (like GDCefGlue's EmbeddedWindow) - since Win WebView2 doesn't support OSR
2. **Position sync** every frame via `_Process` - like GDCefGlue's `ProcessEmbeddedMode` and godot_wry's `update_webview`
3. **JS injection** for input forwarding - like godot_wry's `forward_input_events`
4. **WebMessage** for JS ↔ C# IPC - WebView2's built-in messaging
5. **res:// protocol** - intercept navigation via `NavigationStarting` event

set; } = true;\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// 自定义 User-Agent\n        /// </summary>\n        [Export]\n        public string UserAgent { get; set; } = \"\";\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// 是否启用缩放快捷键 (Ctrl++/-)\n        /// </summary>\n        [Export]\n        public bool ZoomHotkeysEnabled { get; 中捕获的鼠标/键盘事件转发到 Godot 输入系统。\n    ///\n    /// 直接参考 godot_wry (lib.rs) 的 JS IPC input 事件处理逻辑：\n    /// - _mouse_move → InputEventMouseMotion\n    /// - _mouse_down/_mouse_up → InputEventMouseButton\n    /// - _mouse_wheel → InputEventMouseButton (WHEEL_UP/WHEEL_DOWN)\n    /// - _key_down/_key_up → InputEventKey\n    /// </summary>\n    internal static class InputEventRouter\n    {\n        private static MouseButtonMask _currentButtonMask = MouseButtonMask.None;\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// 尝试将 WebView IPC 消息作为输入事件处理。\n        /// 返回 true 表示已处理（是输入事件），false 表示是普通 IPC 消息。\n        /// </summary>\n        public static bool TryProcessInputEvent(string json, Control target)\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                var jsonObj = Json.ParseString(json);\n                if (jsonObj.VariantType != Variant.Type.Dictionary)\n                    return false;\n\n                var dict = jsonObj.AsGodotDictionary();\n                if (!dict.ContainsKey(\"type\"))\n                    return false;\n\n                string type = dict[\"type\"].AsString();\n                if (!type.StartsWith(\"_\"))\n                    return false;\n\n                var viewport = target.GetViewport();\n                if (viewport == null) return true; // 消费掉，但不是有效事件\n\n                float x = dict.GetValueOrDefault(\"x\", 0.0f);\n                float y = dict.GetValueOrDefault(\"y\", 0.0f);\n                var globalPos = target.GlobalPosition;\n                float vpX = globalPos.X + x;\n                float vpY = globalPos.Y + y;\n\n                switch (type)\n                {\n                    case \"_mouse_move\":\n                        return HandleMouseMove(dict, vpX, vpY, viewport);\n\n                    case \"_mouse_down\":\n                    case \"_mouse_up\":\n                        return HandleMouseButton(type, dict, vpX, vpY, viewport);\n\n                    case \"_mouse_wheel\":\n                        return  private static bool HandleMouseMove(Dictionary dict, float vpX, float vpY, Viewport viewport)\n        {\n            float movementX = dict.GetValueOrDefault(\"movementX\", 0.0f);\n            float GlobalPosition = new Vector2(vpX, vpY),\n                ButtonMask = _currentButtonMask,\n                Relative = new Vector2(movementX, movementY)\n            };\n            viewport.PushInput(ev);\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        private static bool HandleMouseButton(string type, Dictionary dict, float vpX, float vpY, Viewport viewport)\n        {\n            int button = dict.GetValueOrDefault(\"button\", 0);\n            var godotButton = MapMouseButton(button);\n            bool pressed = type == \"_mouse_down\";\n\n            // 更新按钮掩码\n viewport.PushInput(ev);\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        private static bool HandleMouseWheel(Dictionary dict, float vpX, float vpY, Viewport viewport)\n        {\n            float 0.0f)\n            {\n                var button = deltaY < 0.0f ? MouseButton.WheelUp : MouseButton.WheelDown;\n                float factor = Math.Max(Math.Abs(deltaY) / 100.0f, 1.0f);\n                SendWheelEvent(button, position, factor, viewport);\n            }\n\n            if (deltaX != 0.0f)\n            {\n                var button = deltaX < 0.0f ? MouseButton.WheelLeft : MouseButton.WheelRight;\n                float factor = Math.Max(Math.Abs(deltaX) / 100.0f, 1.0f);\n                SendWheelEvent(button, position, factor, viewport);\n            }\n\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        private static void SendWheelEvent(MouseButton button, Vector2 position, float factor, Viewport viewport)\n        {\n            foreach (bool pressed in new[] { true, false })\n            {\n                var ev = new InputEventMouseButton\n                {\n                    ButtonIndex = button,\n                    Position = position,\n                    GlobalPosition = position,\n                    Pressed = pressed,\n                    Factor = factor,\n                    ButtonMask = _currentButtonMask\n                };\n                viewport.PushInput(ev);\n            }\n        }\n\n        private static bool HandleKeyboard(string type, Dictionary dict, Viewport viewport)\n        {\n            string keyStr = dict.GetValueOrDefault(\"key\", \"\");\n            var godotKey = MapGodotKey(keyStr);\n\n            var ev = new InputEventKey\n            {\n                Keycode = godotKey,\n }

│   ├── WindowsWebViewBackend.cs              ← Windows WebView2 实现
│   └── InputEventRouter.cs                   ← 输入事件转发
│
└── Demo/
    ├── DemoBrowser.cs                        ← 演示脚本
    ├── DemoBrowser.tscn                      ← 演示场景
    └── html/index.html                       ← 演示 HTML 页面
```

### 对标 godot_wry 的功能覆盖

| 功能 | godot_wry (Rust) | GodotWebViewSharp (C#) |
|------|------------------|----------------------|
| **系统浏览器** | WRY (WebView2/WebKit) | WebView2 (Windows) |
| **窗口嵌入** | `build_webview` + `reparent_webview` | `SetParent` + 每帧 `SetWindowPos` |
| **位置同步** | `update_webview` 检测变化 | `SyncPosition` 检测变化（参考 GDCefGlue） |
| **输入转发** | JS IPC `_mouse_move/key` 等 | JS 注入 `chrome.webview.postMessage` |
| **IPC 信号** | `ipc_message` | `IpcMessage` signal |
| **页面加载信号** | `page_load_started/finished` | `PageLoadStarted/Finished` signal |
| **JS 执行** | `eval(script)` | `EvaluateJavaScript(script)` |
| **C#→JS 消息** | `post_message(msg)` | `PostMessage(msg)` → CustomEvent |
| **res:// 协议** | 自定义 protocol 处理器 | `ResolveUrl` → file:// 映射 |
| **DevTools** | `open_devtools/close_devtools` | `OpenDevTools/CloseDevTools` |
| **全窗口模式** | `full_window_size` | `FullWindowSize` |
| **属性** | url/html/user_agent/devtools/transparent 等 | 完整对应导出属性 |

### 三个参考源的借鉴点

| 参考源 | 借鉴内容 |
|--------|----------|
| **godot_wry** | 窗口嵌入逻辑、JS 输入事件全映射（mouse/key/wheel）、`GODOT_KEYS` 映射表、IPC 模式 |
| **GDCefGlue** | C# Godot 插件结构、`ProcessEmbeddedMode` 位置变化检测、`NativeWindowMethods` 模式、`[GlobalClass]` 注册 |
| **604Assembly** | WebView2 初始化流程、`SetParent` 嵌入、`FindWindowEx("Chrome_WidgetWin_0")` 获取子窗口 |

### 下一步操作

1. **用 Godot 4.6+ 打开 `src/project.godot`** — 自动识别 `WebViewControl` 为可拖拽节点
2. **构建后运行** DemoBrowser 场景，体验浏览器功能
3. **跨平台扩展**：macOS → 仿照 `WindowsWebViewBackend` 实现 `MacWebViewBackend`（使用 WKWebView 原生框架）

`AutoConfigUrl`, embedded proxy credentials, and direct PDF export are not currently applied there. On Android, the official AndroidX proxy override remains app-wide, so per-instance proxy configuration is still contract-only on the current runtime path. Browser targets remain unsupported for per-instance proxy application because the host browser does not expose per-instance engine proxy control.`WebAuthenticationBroker.UseHttpPost

...
Use`NativeWebViewPlatformImplementationStatusMatrix.Get(platform)` to inspect the honest current repo runtime status for each target. Use`NativeWebViewProxyPlatformSupportMatrix.Get(platform)` for proxy-specific status. The current core package also exposes`NativeWebViewWindowsProxyArgumentsBuilder` and`NativeWebViewLinuxProxySettingsBuilder` so future or custom backends can translate shared proxy options into WebView2/WebKitGTK-specific configuration payloads without duplicating parsing logic. Exact`UserDataFolder`/`CacheFolder`/`CookieDataFolder`/`SessionDataFolder` behavior remains backend-specific. In the current repo, Linux currently runtime-applies`CookieDataFolder`,`Language`,`IsInPrivateModeEnabled`, and explicit proxy settings on the embedded control path, while`UserDataFolder`,`CacheFolder`, and`SessionDataFolder` remain backend-specific configuration contracts there. The macOS and iOS`WKWebView` proxy/runtime paths use storage-path values as part of isolated data-store identity rather than direct physical directory mapping.

```
using NativeWebView.Controls;
using NativeWebView.Core;
...
Current embedded runtime implementation exists on Windows, macOS, and Linux in the default desktop build. iOS and Android runtime support is compiled from their platform-targeted backend assemblies rather than the default`net10.0` contract build, and the Browser runtime is compiled from the browser-targeted backend assembly. Windows and Linux now also ship real`NativeWebDialog` runtimes backed by native desktop windows plus their existing WebView2/WebKitGTK engines, and`WebAuthenticationBroker` runs real modal or popup browser sessions whenever the corresponding platform runtime assembly is present. The Browser implementation hosts a real embedded`iframe` through Avalonia Browser native hosting and a DOM bridge; navigation is real, but script execution,`window.chrome.webview` emulation, and new-window interception depend on same-origin access or explicit`postMessage` cooperation from the hosted page, and normal browser frame restrictions such as`X-Frame-Options`/`Content-Security-Policy: frame-ancestors` still apply. Browser authentication uses`window.open`, so popup support plus an inspectable`http` or`https` callback URL are required there; custom-scheme callbacks and`UseHttpPost` are not supported on the current browser runtime path. The iOS implementation hosts`WKWebView` through a backend-owned`UIView` attachment path and a modal authentication controller when`NativeWebView.Platform.iOS` is built with the .NET 10 Apple workload;`NativeWebDialog` remains unsupported there. The Android implementation hosts`android.webkit.WebView` through a backend-owned child`View` attachment and a dedicated authentication activity when`NativeWebView.Platform.Android` is built with the .NET 10 Android workload;`NativeWebDialog` remains unsupported there. Per-instance proxy application is effective on the Windows and Linux`NativeWebView` and`NativeWebDialog` runtime paths, on macOS 14+ for`NativeWebView` and`NativeWebDialog`, and on iOS 17+ for the embedded`NativeWebView` control when the iOS runtime assembly is present. The macOS and iOS implementations support explicit`http`,`https`, and`socks5` proxy servers, credentials, and bypass domains, and use a dedicated`WKWebsiteDataStore` identity derived from the instance configuration so proxied views do not fall back to private-browsing storage semantics. On Linux, explicit`http`,`https`, and`socks` proxies are runtime-applied on the X11 control/dialog paths;`AutoConfigUrl`, embedded proxy credentials, and direct PDF export are not currently applied there. On Android, the official AndroidX proxy override remains app-wide, so per-instance proxy configuration is still contract-only on the current runtime path. Browser targets remain unsupported for per-instance proxy application because the host browser does not expose per-instance engine proxy control.`WebAuthenticationBroker.UseHttpPost` currently remains unsupported on all runtime backends in this repo.
...
Use`NativeWebViewPlatformImplementationStatusMatrix.Get(platform)` to inspect the honest current repo runtime status for each target. Use`NativeWebViewProxyPlatformSupportMatrix.Get(platform)` for proxy-specific status. The current core package also exposes`NativeWebViewWindowsProxyArgumentsBuilder` and`NativeWebViewLinuxProxySettingsBuilder` so future or custom backends can translate shared proxy options into WebView2/WebKitGTK-specific configuration payloads without duplicating parsing logic. Exact`UserDataFolder`/`CacheFolder`/`CookieDataFolder`/`SessionDataFolder` behavior remains backend-specific. In the current repo, Linux currently runtime-applies`CookieDataFolder`,`Language`,`IsInPrivateModeEnabled`, and explicit proxy settings on the embedded control path, while`UserDataFolder`,`CacheFolder`, and`SessionDataFolder` remain backend-specific configuration contracts there. The macOS and iOS`WKWebView` proxy/runtime paths use storage-path values as part of isolated data-store identity rather than direct physical directory mapping.
```

## NativeWebView

Native webview stack for Avalonia that stays on top of platform-native engines instead of bundling Chromium.

### NuGet Packages

End-user installs are typically NativeWebView plus the platform package for the target runtime. NativeWebView.Dialog and NativeWebView.Auth are optional facades, while Core and Interop are primarily composition units and transitive dependencies.

#### Package Layout

| Package | NuGet | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- |
| NativeWebView | Avalonia control facade API. |
| NativeWebView.Core | Shared contracts, controllers, feature model, and backend factory. |
| NativeWebView.Dialog | Dialog facade API. |
| NativeWebView.Auth | Web authentication broker facade API. |
| NativeWebView.Interop | Native handle contracts and structs. |
| NativeWebView.Platform.Windows | Windows backend registration and implementation. |
| NativeWebView.Platform.macOS | macOS backend registration and implementation. |
| NativeWebView.Platform.Linux | Linux backend registration and implementation. |
| NativeWebView.Platform.iOS | iOS backend registration and implementation. |
| NativeWebView.Platform.Android | Android backend registration and implementation. |
| NativeWebView.Platform.Browser | Browser backend registration and implementation. |

### Features

* NativeWebView control for embedded native browser surfaces inside Avalonia.
* NativeWebDialog facade for dialog and popup browser workflows.
* WebAuthenticationBroker facade for OAuth and interactive sign-in.
* Platform backends for Windows, macOS, Linux, iOS, Android, and Browser.
* Optional airspace-mitigation modes: Embedded, GpuSurface, and Offscreen.
* Diagnostics, capability reporting, render-frame export, integrity metadata, and smoke-testable sample apps.

### Current Repo Runtime Status

| Platform | NativeWebView control | NativeWebDialog | WebAuthenticationBroker | Per-instance proxy |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Windows | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented |
| macOS | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented on macOS 14+ |
| Linux | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented | Implemented |
| iOS | Implemented when built with the .NET 8 Apple workload | Unsupported | Implemented when built with the .NET 8 Apple workload | Implemented on iOS 17+ when built with the .NET 8 Apple workload |
| Android | Implemented when built with the .NET 8 Android workload | Unsupported | Implemented when built with the .NET 8 Android workload | Contract-only, app-wide platform API only |
| Browser | Implemented when built for the browser target | Unsupported | Implemented when built for the browser target | Unsupported |

Features and registered backend modules continue to describe platform capability contracts. Use NativeWebViewPlatformImplementationStatusMatrix.Get(platform) for the honest current repo runtime status, and NativeWebViewProxyPlatformSupportMatri

| **macOS** | WKWebView (Safari) | 原生框架互操作 (`WebKit` framework via P/Invoke) |
| **Linux** | WebKitGTK | 原生库互操作 (`libwebkitgtk` via P/Invoke) |
| **Android** | `android.webkit.WebView` | Godot Android plugin + .NET Android binding |
| **iOS** | WKWebView | Godot iOS plugin + .NET iOS binding |

## NativeWebView 的依赖分析

```
NativeWebView (Avalonia 控件外观)       ← 需要 Avalonia UI 框架
NativeWebView.Core (共享合约)           ← 设计上依附 Avalonia 生态
NativeWebView.Platform.Windows         ← 依赖 Avalonia 窗口系统
NativeWebView.Platform.macOS/Linux     ← 同上
NativeWebView.Platform.Android         ← 依赖 Avalonia.Android
NativeWebView.Platform.iOS             ← 依赖 Avalonia.iOS
```

`NativeWebView` 整个栈是为 **Avalonia**（一个独立的 UI 框架）设计的。它的 Platform 后端期望的是 Avalonia 的窗口系统（`TopLevel`, `Window` 等），与 Godot 的 `Control`/`DisplayServer` 完全不同。强行引用会拖入整个 Avalonia 依赖和架构冲突。

## 正确做法：架构参考，不引用

我已经按这个思路设计了项目。这是跨平台扩展计划：

| 平台 | 系统 WebView | 实现方案 | 状态 |
|------|-------------|---------|------|
| **Windows** | WebView2 (Edge Chromium) | `Microsoft.Web.WebView2` NuGet | ✅ **已实现** |
| **macOS** | WKWebView (WebKit) | 原生框架 DllImport (`/System/Library/Frameworks/WebKit.framework`) | ⏳ 待实现 |
| **Linux** | WebKitGTK | 原生库 DllImport (`libwebkitgtk`) | ⏳ 待实现 |
| **Android** | `android.webkit.WebView` | Godot Android Plugin + .NET Android 绑定 | ⏳ 未来 |
| **iOS** | WKWebView | Godot iOS Plugin + .NET iOS 绑定 | ⏳ 未来 |

### 如何借鉴 NativeWebView

`IWebViewBackend` 接口已经为你留好了扩展点。对于 macOS/Linux/Android，参考 NativeWebView 的 **[源码实现](https://github.com/wieslawsoltes/NativeWebView/tree/main/src)**：

```csharp
// NativeWebView 源码位置 → 我们的借鉴
src/NativeWebView.Platform.Windows/WindowsNativeWebViewBackend.cs  → 已完成
src/NativeWebView.Platform.macOS/MacNativeWebViewBackend.cs       → 参考 WKWebView 创建和 JS bridge
src/NativeWebView.Platform.Linux/LinuxNativeWebViewBackend.cs     → 参考 WebKitGTK 嵌入
src/NativeWebView.Platform.Android/AndroidNativeWebViewBackend.cs → 参考 android.webkit.WebView 集成
```

### 当前的 IWebViewBackend 接口已经为跨平台设计

```csharp
internal interface IWebViewBackend : IDisposable
{
    Task CreateAsync(Control hostControl);  // 传入 Godot Control
    void SetBounds(int x, int y, int w, int h);
    void Navigate(string url);
    void NavigateToString(string html);
    void ExecuteScript(string script);
    void PostMessage(string message);
    // ... 各平台各自实现
}
```

对于 **Android 平台**，需要额外做：
1. Godot Android 插件项目（`.aar`），使用 `android.webkit.WebView`
2. 通过 Godot 的 `GodotAndroidPlugin` 接口暴露到 C#
3. 渲染方式使用 `EditorExportPlugin` 导出配置

想继续实现某个平台的 backend 吗？或者先把 Windows 端打磨完善？

## 原因分析

`Cannot get class 'WebViewControl'` — 这个错误的根本原因是：**Godot 编辑器还没编译过 C# 项目**，`[GlobalClass]` 注册的类还没被加载到 Godot 中。

## 解决步骤

### 1. 打开 Godot 编辑器

运行 `open_godot.bat`（刚创建的），或直接：

```
D:\APP\Godot_v4.6.3-stable_mono_win64\Godot_v4.6.3-stable_mono_win64.exe
```

在 Project Manager 中点击 **Import** → 选择 `E:\Work\Hub\GodotWebViewSharp\src\project.godot`

### 2. 构建 C# 项目

打开项目后，点击右上角的 **Build** 按钮（或菜单 `Project → Build`）：

![Build button](https://docs.godotengine.org/en/stable/_images/build_button.png)

Godot 会自动执行 `dotnet build`，编译完成后：
- `WebViewControl` 被 `[GlobalClass]` 注册
- 场景中的 `WebViewControl` 节点可以被正常实例化
- 拖拽 `WebViewControl` 节点到场景中，所有导出属性会在 Inspector 中显示

### 3. 验证构建

构建完成后，你应该能看到输出类似：

```
GodotWebViewSharp -> .godot/mono/temp/bin/Debug/GodotWebViewSharp.dll
Build completed successfully.
```

---

如果不想手动操作，也可以从命令行构建后再用 Godot 打开：

```
cd E:\Work\Hub\GodotWebViewSharp\src
dotnet build
D:\APP\Godot_v4.6.3-stable_mono_win64\Godot_v4.6.3-stable_mono_win64.exe --path .
```

> **注意**：`<Godot.NET.Sdk Version="4.6.3">` 与你的 Godot 4.6.3 版本匹配。如果以后升级 Godot，记得同步更新 `.csproj` 中的版本号。

# sv

Everything you need to build a Svelte project, powered by [`sv`](https://github.com/sveltejs/cli).

## Creating a project

If you're seeing this, you've probably already done this step. Congrats!

```bash
# create a new project in the current directory
npx sv create

# create a new project in my-app
npx sv create my-app
```

## Developing

Once you've created a project and installed dependencies with `npm install` (or `pnpm install` or `yarn`), start a development server:

```bash
npm run dev

# or start the server and open the app in a new browser tab
npm run dev -- --open
```

## Building

To create a production version of your app:

```bash
npm run build
```

You can preview the production build with `npm run preview`.

> To deploy your app, you may need to install an [adapter](https://svelte.dev/docs/kit/adapters) for your target environment.

```

完成。给你总结一下做的所有事情：

## 1. 修复了 `DemoBrowser.tscn`

- 用 **GDScript 辅助脚本**（`script = SubResource`）替代纯 C# 方案，这样**不用先 Build 就能打开场景**
- 添加了完整的信号连接：后退/前进/刷新/Go/DevTools 按钮、URL 输入框
- 默认加载 `res://Demo/html/index.html`（我们的 demo 页面）
- **注意**：Godot 编辑器已经打开过这个场景，把 `WebView` 节点存为普通 `Control` + 脚本引用（而不是 `type="WebViewControl"`），**这种写法不用 `[GlobalClass]` 也能工作**。等构建完成后，可以改为 `type="WebViewControl"`。

## 2. 从 godot_wry 复制了 demo 文件

| 源 | 目标 | 说明 |
|----|------|------|
| `test.html` | `src/Demo/godot_wry_test.html` | 简单 `res://` 测试页面 |
| `ui/build/*` | `src/Demo/character_creator/ui/` | 角色创建器 Svelte Web UI |
| `assets/*` | `src/Demo/character_creator/assets/` | 3D 角色模型 + 材质 + 纹理 |
| `character_creator.gd` | `src/Demo/character_creator/` | IPC 控制脚本（GDScript） |

**角色创建器 demo** 的工作方式：
- Web UI（在 WebView 中加载）通过 `window.chrome.webview.postMessage` 发送 IPC 消息
- Godot 端的 `ipc_message` signal 接收消息 → 控制 3D 角色换装/换色

> **注意**：角色创建器的 3D 场景文件（`.tscn`，1500+行包含 mesh 数据）没有复制，因为路径和材质引用都需要对应调整。如果你想跑这个 demo，需要在 Godot 编辑器中导入 `character.glb` 后重建场景节点树，或者直接从 godot_wry 复制整个 `character_creator.tscn` 并批量替换路径。

### 使用方式

打开 Godot 4.6.3 → Import `src/project.godot` → **Build**（先构建 C#）→ 运行 `DemoBrowser.tscn` → 看到 WebView 加载了 demo 页面

不过需要说明的是：`WebViewControl` 的 `_Ready()` 里会尝试创建 WebView2 窗口，这在编辑器中会失败（没有运行时窗口句柄）。所以 `[Tool]` 模式下需要在 `_Ready()` 里加个保护：

```csharp
public override void _Ready()
{
    if (Engine.IsEditorHint())
        return;  // 编辑器模式下不创建 WebView2
    _isReady = true;
    InitializeWebView();
}
```

```csharp
public override void _Ready()
{
    if (Engine.IsEditorHint())
        return;  // 编辑器下不创建 WebView

    _isReady = true;
    InitializeWebView();
}
```

`_Process()` 里也加了同样保护，这样编辑器中放 `WebViewControl` 节点不会有任何副作用。Build 后丢到场景里，Inspector 中所有导出属性都可见可调。
